!Sample_data_processing = Post-sequencing quality control using three quality control metrics: 1) Percent of reads allocated to the Positive control probe must not exceed 15%, 2) Total aligned reads must exceed 1,500,000, and 3) Relative Standard Deviaion (CV) of counts within a sample must exceed 0.1 (CV is calculated after removal of counts from ANT and POS control probes and log2 transformation after addition of 2 to avoid taking the log of 0). A log2 CPM normalization is then processed.

!Sample_data_processing = Post-sequencing quality control using three quality control metrics: 1) Percent of reads allocated to the Positive control probe must not exceed 15%, 2) Total aligned reads must exceed 1,500,000, and 3) Relative Standard Deviaion (CV) of counts within a sample must exceed 0.1 (CV is calculated after removal of counts from ANT and POS control probes and log2 transformation after addition of 2 to avoid taking the log of 0). A log2 CPM normalization is then processed.

!Sample_data_processing = Post-sequencing quality control using three quality control metrics: 1) Percent of reads allocated to the Positive control probe must not exceed 15%, 2) Total aligned reads must exceed 1,500,000, and 3) Relative Standard Deviaion (CV) of counts within a sample must exceed 0.1 (CV is calculated after removal of counts from ANT and POS control probes and log2 transformation after addition of 2 to avoid taking the log of 0). A log2 CPM normalization is then processed.

!Sample_data_processing = Post-sequencing quality control using three quality control metrics: 1) Percent of reads allocated to the Positive control probe must not exceed 15%, 2) Total aligned reads must exceed 1,500,000, and 3) Relative Standard Deviaion (CV) of counts within a sample must exceed 0.1 (CV is calculated after removal of counts from ANT and POS control probes and log2 transformation after addition of 2 to avoid taking the log of 0). A log2 CPM normalization is then processed.

!Sample_data_processing = Post-sequencing quality control using three quality control metrics: 1) Percent of reads allocated to the Positive control probe must not exceed 15%, 2) Total aligned reads must exceed 1,500,000, and 3) Relative Standard Deviaion (CV) of counts within a sample must exceed 0.1 (CV is calculated after removal of counts from ANT and POS control probes and log2 transformation after addition of 2 to avoid taking the log of 0). A log2 CPM normalization is then processed.

!Sample_data_processing = Post-sequencing quality control using three quality control metrics: 1) Percent of reads allocated to the Positive control probe must not exceed 15%, 2) Total aligned reads must exceed 1,500,000, and 3) Relative Standard Deviaion (CV) of counts within a sample must exceed 0.1 (CV is calculated after removal of counts from ANT and POS control probes and log2 transformation after addition of 2 to avoid taking the log of 0). A log2 CPM normalization is then processed.

!Sample_data_processing = Post-sequencing quality control using three quality control metrics: 1) Percent of reads allocated to the Positive control probe must not exceed 15%, 2) Total aligned reads must exceed 1,500,000, and 3) Relative Standard Deviaion (CV) of counts within a sample must exceed 0.1 (CV is calculated after removal of counts from ANT and POS control probes and log2 transformation after addition of 2 to avoid taking the log of 0). A log2 CPM normalization is then processed.
